}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.502
	title = ita_dnc.502.t
	desc = ita_dnc.502.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = ITA_w_double_book
		NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_w_note_sealed }
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.502.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_vienna_trust_down_l
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_w_the_denial
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_political_power = -25
		set_country_flag = ITA_w_the_denial
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 20 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = ITA_vienna_trust
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.502.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_vienna_trust_down_l
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		add_political_power = -50
		add_war_support = 0.05
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = ITA_w_the_double_book
			add_idea = ITA_w_the_broken_word
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 40 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = ITA_vienna_trust
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.600
	title = ita_dnc.600.t
	desc = ita_dnc.600.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_strike
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = ITA_p_the_strike_spreads
				days = 60
			}
			add_stability = -0.03
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.600.a
		trigger = {
			has_political_power > 59
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_idea = ITA_a_the_state_takes_no_side
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -60
		add_war_support = -0.03
		ITA_piazza_concede = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.600.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_idea = ITA_a_the_state_of_siege
			}
		}
		ITA_piazza_repress = yes
		remove_ideas = ITA_p_the_strike_spreads
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.600.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		remove_ideas = ITA_p_the_strike_spreads
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = ITA_p_the_strike_spreads
			days = 120
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 4 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 5 }
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.602
	title = ita_dnc.602.t
	desc = ita_dnc.602.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_riot
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			ITA_p_ungovern_two_states = yes
			add_stability = -0.06
			set_country_flag = ITA_p_the_provinces_are_out
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.602.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
		}
		ITA_piazza_repress = yes
		ITA_piazza_repress = yes
		add_stability = -0.08
		add_war_support = -0.10
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.604
			days = 90
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.602.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 35
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 18 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 20 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_prefects value = 25 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_a_the_prefects_circular }
			remove_ideas = ITA_a_the_prefects_circular
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_the_prefect_machine }
			remove_ideas = ITA_the_prefect_machine
		}
		ITA_machine_reckon = yes
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.604
			days = 60
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.602.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				country_event = {
					id = ita_dnc.604
					days = 120
				}
			}
			50 = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 12 }
				add_to_variable = { var = ITA_piazza_high_months value = 2 }
				hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = ITA_p_the_provinces_are_left }
				ITA_piazza_bands = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.603
	title = ita_dnc.603.t
	desc = ita_dnc.603.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_gathering_protest
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = ITA_p_the_insurrection
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.15
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.603.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_cw_the_romagna_rises_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_cw_tt_side_the_romagna
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_war_support = -0.05
		add_ideas = ITA_p_the_country_divided
		set_variable = { ITA_piazza = 55 }
		set_variable = { ITA_piazza_high_months = 0 }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 25 }
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		set_country_flag = ITA_r_civil_war
		start_civil_war = {
			ideology = communism
			size = 0.35
			states = { 161 162 157 }
			keep_political_leader = yes
			keep_political_party_members = yes
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				original_tag = ITA
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_character = ITA_errico_malatesta } }
				ITA_errico_malatesta = { set_nationality = PREV }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_errico_malatesta ideology = anarcho_syndicalism }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_character = ITA_arturo_labriola } }
				ITA_arturo_labriola = { set_nationality = PREV }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_arturo_labriola ideology = syndicalism }
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.701
	title = ita_dnc.701.t
	desc = ita_dnc.701.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_election_vote
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.701.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_majority_bought_up_l
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_piazza_up_m
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ITA_machine_now
				value = 30
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority_bought value = 12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 6 }
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.705
			days = 20
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.701.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_ita_dnc_701_b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ITA_piazza
					value = 40
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 6 }
		}
		else = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 4 }
		}
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.705
			days = 20
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.701.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
		}
		add_political_power = -60
		add_stability = 0.04
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 6 }
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = democratic
			popularity = 0.04
		}
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.705
			days = 20
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.702
	title = ita_dnc.702.t
	desc = ita_dnc.702.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_election_vote
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.702.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_majority_bought_up_l
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_piazza_up_m
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ITA_machine_now
				value = 30
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority_bought value = 12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 6 }
		ITA_piazza_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.705
			days = 20
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.702.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_ita_dnc_702_b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_a_the_catholic_vote }
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_t_the_gentiloni_understanding }
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 10 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 2 }
		}
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ita_dnc.705
			days = 20
		}

	desc = ita_dnc.2101.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_ITA_vittorio_emanuele_III
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = ITA_ch_monza_happened
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_umberto_i }
				retire_character = ITA_umberto_i
			}
			promote_character = { character = ITA_vittorio_emanuele_iii ideology = monarchism }
			add_country_leader_trait = ITA_the_presidency
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = ITA_umberto }
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = ITA_umberto
					add_idea = ITA_t_the_oath_of_the_new_reign
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2101.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_crown_up_l
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_sonnino }
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 55 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 8 }
		ITA_crown_bands = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_sonnino
				has_country_flag = ITA_s_crown_flinched
			}
			country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2102 days = 6 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2101.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_crown_down_m
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_majority_up_s
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_giolitti }
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_reformist }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 5 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_majority value = 3 }
		ITA_crown_bands = yes
		ITA_chamber_bands = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_sonnino
				has_country_flag = ITA_s_crown_flinched
			}
			country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2102 days = 6 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2101.c
		trigger = {
			has_political_power > 49
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_giolitti
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_concordat
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_dec_amnesty_new_reign_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 10 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_piazza_floor value = 3 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 3 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = ITA_piazza_floor
				min = 0
				max = 45
			}
			ITA_piazza_bands = yes
			ITA_crown_bands = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_sonnino
				has_country_flag = ITA_s_crown_flinched
			}
			country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2102 days = 6 }
		}
	}
}

	title = ita_dnc.1879.t
	desc = ita_dnc.1879.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_read_write
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1879.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_government = neutrality }
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { has_country_flag = ITA_r_king_gone } }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = large_decrease }
		ITA = {
			add_named_threat = { threat = 1 name = ITA_r_threat_berlin_will_not_recognise }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1879.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_opinion = { target = ITA value > 0 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_war = yes }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_increase }
	}
}

			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = AUH_dnc_path_sword }
			modifier = { factor = 0.3 has_war = yes }
		}
		create_wargoal = {
			type = topple_government
			target = ITA

		add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = ITA_r_threat_the_tyrol_corps_are_massed }

			set_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 0 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1884.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 ITA = { check_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 30 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }

country_event = {
	id = ita_dnc.1886
	title = ita_dnc.1886.t
	desc = ita_dnc.1886.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_conference
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1886.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_vienna_trust_up_l
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_r_irredenta_renounced
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 50 compare = less_than } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = ITA_r_entente }
		}
		set_country_flag = ITA_r_irredenta_renounced
		add_to_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 20 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = ITA_vienna_trust
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = medium_increase } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1886.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_tt_vienna_trust_set_0
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 check_variable = { var = ITA_piazza value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = ITA_r_syndicalists_radical }
		}
		set_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = AUH }
			AUH = {
				create_wargoal = {
					type = topple_government
					target = ITA
				}
				add_ai_strategy = { type = antagonize id = ITA value = 100 }
				add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = ITA_r_threat_the_tyrol_corps_are_massed }
			}
		}
	}

			base = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				surrender_progress > 0.3

			}

		add_stability = -0.05

			transfer_state = 867

			transfer_state = 993

			white_peace = AUH
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = GER }
			GER = { country_event = { id = ita_dnc.1674 days = 5 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.1672.b
		trigger = {
			has_war_with = ITA
			surrender_progress > 0.6
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1

			939 = { add_core_of = ITA }

		if = {

				country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2365 days = 140 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = ITADNC_h_the_ministry_of_industry }
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = ITADNC_h_the_plenipotentiary_of_credit }
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = ITADNC_h_the_plenipotentiary_of_the_treasury }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ITA_h_treasury_prevailed = 2 } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { ITA_h_treasury_prevailed = 3 } }
				}
				activate_shine_on_focus = ITADNC_h_the_ministry_of_industry
				set_country_flag = { flag = ITA_h_the_ministry_of_industry_in_progress_flag days = 141 value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2365 days = 140 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = ITADNC_h_the_ministry_of_industry
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = ITADNC_h_the_new_industries }
					NOT = { has_shine_effect_on_focus = ITADNC_h_the_new_industries }

				set_country_flag = { flag = ITA_h_the_new_industries_in_progress_flag days = 281 value = 1 }
				set_variable = { ITA_h_new_industries_focus_minister = 1 }

		}

			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_opinion = {
					target = FROM
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_war_with = FROM
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = GAME_OVER_TT
		hidden_effect = {
			set_global_flag = ITA_o_the_iberian_union_is_made
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_o_the_union_inherits_the_wars_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = { has_defensive_war_with = ROOT }
				FROM = { declare_war_on = { target = PREV type = annex_everything } }
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = { has_offensive_war_with = ROOT }
				declare_war_on = { target = FROM type = annex_everything }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			annex_country = {
				target = ROOT
				transfer_troops = yes
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				add_core_of = FROM
			}
		}
		ITA = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_faction_leader = yes
					FROM = {
						is_in_faction = no
					}
				}
				add_to_faction = FROM
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_o_the_union_stands_alone_tt
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2548.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		clr_global_flag = ITA_o_the_iberian_union_is_open
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = FROM
			modifier = medium_decrease
		}
	}
}

	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			random_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = ITA_cw_state_taken }

				if = {

		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_political_power = 25
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = ITA_luigi_pelloux ruling_only = yes } }
			ITA_luigi_pelloux = { retire = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_sidney_sonnino }
				ITA_sidney_sonnino = {
					add_country_leader_role = {
						country_leader = {
							ideology = oligarchism
							expire = "1923.1.1.1"
						}
						promote_leader = yes
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_umberto_i }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_umberto_i ideology = monarchism }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_vittorio_emanuele_iii }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_vittorio_emanuele_iii ideology = monarchism }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { character = ITA_tancredi_saletta ruling_only = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_giuseppe_zanardelli }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_giuseppe_zanardelli ideology = liberalism }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_luigi_luzzatti }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_luigi_luzzatti ideology = liberalism }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_giovanni_giolitti }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_giovanni_giolitti ideology = democratic_socialism }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = ITA_tancredi_saletta ruling_only = yes } } }
				remove_country_leader_role = { character = ITA_tancredi_saletta ideology = democratic_militarism }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ITA_u_the_provisional_regime }
			remove_ideas = ITA_u_the_provisional_regime
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_cw_the_general_retired_tt
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = ITA_cw_the_general_retired }
	}
}

				factor = 3
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_cw_ai_constitutional
					has_country_flag = ITA_cw_ai_oath

				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_cw_ai_republic
					has_game_rule = { rule = ITA_dnc_ai_behavior option = ITA_REPUBLIC }
				}
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_cw_tt_2986_a

			limit = { has_country_flag = ITA_cw_entrance_comando }
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_crown value = 10 tooltip = ITA_tt_crown_up_l }
			clamp_variable = { var = ITA_crown min = 0 max = 100 }
			ITA_crown_bands = yes
			add_stability = 0.05
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ITA_ch_monza_happened }
					NOT = { has_idea = ITA_umberto }
				}
				add_ideas = ITA_umberto
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_ch_monza_happened
					NOT = { has_idea = ITA_t_the_oath_of_the_new_reign }
				}
				add_ideas = ITA_t_the_oath_of_the_new_reign
			}
			set_country_flag = ITA_cw_the_king_signed
		}
		else = {
			complete_national_focus = ITADNC_o_recall_the_chamber
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2986.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ITA_cw_ai_republic
					has_game_rule = { rule = ITA_dnc_ai_behavior option = ITA_REPUBLIC }
				}
			}
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_leader = { character = ITA_filippo_turati ruling_only = yes } }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_cw_tt_2986_b
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = ITA_umberto_i }
			retire_character = ITA_umberto_i
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.05 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.05 }
		add_stability = -0.05
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ITA_cw_entrance_comando }
			set_country_flag = ITA_cw_the_king_took_ship
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_vittorio_emanuele_iii }
				retire_character = ITA_vittorio_emanuele_iii
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_napoleone_colajanni }
				promote_character = { character = ITA_napoleone_colajanni ideology = democratic_republicanism }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character = ITA_salvatore_barzilai }
				ITA_salvatore_barzilai = {
					add_country_leader_role = {
						promote_leader = yes
						country_leader = {
							ideology = democratic_republicanism
							expire = "1939.1.1.1"
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = democratic
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = ITA_REP
			add_ideas = ITA_r_the_republic
			ITA_u_the_hundred_days_are_won = yes
		}
	}

	id = ita_dnc.2236
	title = ita_dnc.2236.t
	desc = ita_dnc.2236.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_naval_treaty
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2236.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 2
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { check_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 55 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
			modifier = { factor = 3 ITA = { has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_sonnino } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_concordat } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_country_flag = AUH_dnc_path_sword }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_increase }
		ITA = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 5 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = ITA_vienna_trust
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = ITA_dec_tt_the_roadstead_is_open
			country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2237 days = 3 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ita_dnc.2236.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = AUH_dnc_path_sword }
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { has_country_flag = ITA_dnc_path_nazionale } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { has_country_flag = ITA_z_antivari_base } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 ITA = { check_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 30 compare = less_than } } }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_decrease }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = GER }
			GER = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ITA modifier = small_decrease } }
		}
		ITA = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ITA_vienna_trust value = 5 }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = ITA_vienna_trust
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			country_event = { id = ita_dnc.2238 days = 3 }
		}
	}
}
